## Classification, cost and current-disposition rules

Product means user-visible, safety, persistence or authority contracts. Implementation boundary means a narrow permitted seam/dependency rather than a new feature. Process covers model choice, recovery, validation, staging and acceptance disposition. Report policy/observation does not gain formal controller-ruling status merely by retention.

N03/N09/N10/N13/N14 are process/test-boundary rulings (N10 also permits a narrow UI prop); N01/N02/N04–N08/N11/N15 are product/safety boundaries. N12 is report-documented UI policy, explicitly not a formal controller ruling. P01/P04–P06 are product; P02/P08 dependency process; P03 evidence interpretation; P07 source/manual disposition; P09/P10/P12 model/review process; P11 test-report path/process seam.

No distinct explicit cost is stated for R51: its busy-token/no-cache/no-overlap/no-queue/no-cancellation limits remain; related R17 states waiting/DB latency. N02 tightens N01 without a separately quantified cost. N03 has **no distinct explicit cost**, so inferred avoided repetition is not a quoted cost. N11 inherits N10's two-path/fixture-coupling cost without a distinct numeric cost. N13 is test-scope only; separate behavior/memory cost is N15. P09 initial role line has no amount/explicit downside; primary28 supplies escalation/rework. No financial/token amount is inferred for model choices.

N01 automatic archive residual is **STILL CURRENT** after Fix4: predictable budget failures write nothing and postarchive validation now errors instead of returning an unadmitted request, but unexpected Store/semantic failures may leave archive/events. Manual Task3 atomicity is separate. N09 conditional context_compaction.rs permission was superseded by N10 concrete seam. N15 retains safe request/pending/known-receipt/error metadata for window lifetime, not restart request-ID persistence or a cross-window lease.

R36 is R12's alias and was never an omission: both original fixed3s/no-timeout-increase texts and completed proof existed. W04 report72–77 records0/2999/3000ms polls and honest existing-correct deadline baseline1PASS, not a manufactured RED or production timeout change. R69/R68 and R28/R78 preserve alias/confirmation history, not extra product choices. R48/R49 earlier unavailable/unstarted status was later followed by old-HEAD isolated partial harness; static browser incomplete then human STOP. Neither is final native acceptance.

P09 exact authorities W08:27–28/31–33 and global202/204: Oct11 writer left reads/no delta, Oct12 fresh standard writer sameBASE, later premium coordinator retained functioning writer. P10 only W08:29; global203 is GUI STOP/source continuation. P11 exact36–37 (38–39 completion/audit); permission only canonical validated workspace target/safe ancestry, never arbitrary destination. ## Exact additional original costs and scope qualifiers

### N01/N02/R47 final prospective cost

Authority `E:/Project/OmicsOps/.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-04-wisp-w05-context-compaction/task-2-report.md:157`:

> ## Prospective-source ruling, proof, cost and limit
> 
> Controller and parent explicitly approved a private same-byte-shape prospective checkpoint source without expanding the Store API. EventStoreV4 archive_context owns the actual archive UUID and push owns timestamps, so future identity equality cannot be claimed before writing. Those fields, archive checksum and management event bodies are filtered out of model context and replay. The projected reference contains only fixed tool/field names, exact future checkpoint sequence (old head + 2), and a hash-verified 64-hex event hash. Source UUID/timestamp/checksum values influence that hash value but not its serialized width. Native budget closures likewise use the prospective post-management sequence and fixed-width hashes. Frozen system/tool/image/provider fields are retained unchanged.
> 
> The native-growth large-science regression verifies complete serialized request byte equality and ModelUsageRequestMetadata equality (serialized_request_bytes is Some), equality of every non-context field, and context equality after substituting only the future checkpoint hash. Returned reference sequence/hash equal the actual persisted checkpoint; the prospective hash differs and is absent from the actual request context. The actual final strict shaper result equals the final admitted request and passes the unchanged 70,000-byte validator. The fixture uses the ModelPort's complete-request serialization/metadata, not an HTTP provider call. Production byte-budget applicability is a scoped source inference: adapters/src/llm.rs RequestBudget documents and implements compact UTF-8 provider-JSON byte counting, with the same retained image shape/bound; replacing one ASCII hex hash with another equal-width hash does not change that estimator. This is not a claim about arbitrary tokenizers or arbitrary future semantic validators.
> 
> Cost: automatic compaction builds two private management envelopes and clones the captured event list; bounded scientific/code views fit once before writes and again with actual durable references. Full admitted contexts avoid extra validation. Existing lower-bound fallback cost/InvalidRequest ambiguity from fix2 remains. Predictable byte-budget failures write nothing. The existing automatic archive+append path is not atomic: unexpected Store failures/races or provider semantic failures after writing may retain an archive/events. Actual bounded request validation then fails and no inadmissible or prospective request is returned for sending. This does not promise rollback or replace future Task3's manual atomic transaction with preallocated actual references.

### N01 Fix4 current residual and actual extra work

Authority `E:/Project/OmicsOps/.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-04-wisp-w05-context-compaction/task-2-report.md:239`:

> The previously skipped full-context archive branch now costs one actual event reload, rerender and complete local validation. This does not add validation to ordinary no-archive fast returns. Predictable prearchive failures still write nothing. Under the parent-approved existing non-atomic automatic path, unexpected Store/semantic failures after archive may leave the archive/events; the function now errors instead of returning an unadmitted request. Manual Task3 atomicity remains separate. No rollback claim or actual closure identity claim is made before its later durable append.

### N15 precise memory/request lifecycle

Authority `E:/Project/OmicsOps/.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-04-wisp-w05-context-compaction/task-5-fix1-report.md:13`:

> A WeakMap keyed by the app Window owns a ledger indexed by full project/conversation/run identity. Each entry stores only the safe request DTO, an unresolved/settled promise flag, the public checked receipt and a boolean error. No prompt, question answer, model context, raw exception, credential, localStorage or database write is introduced. Window listeners are registered/removed with component layout-effect lifetime.
> 
> The first operation synchronously reserves the ledger before invoking native code. A remounted Shell may generate a new seed request ID, but the existing operation retains its original ID. Busy is read synchronously from the shared scoped entry on the first render; remount/hydration never dispatches. Duplicate clicks, including after Back to projects/reopen, make no additional native call. Unknown failure retry reuses the original request identity.
> 
> Generation checks still control visible receipt/error delivery. The original offscreen completion updates only its scoped ledger; it cannot populate another project/conversation/run or a leave-and-return generation. A returned view whose original operation settles after remount can explicitly reveal that checked receipt with its original ID without another native call. A receipt that settled while unmounted is restored on exact-scope hydration, also without dispatch. Only a NEW explicit attempt after visible confirmed success creates a fresh ID. A known receipt remains independent of usage-refresh failure. Pending question/history and the existing Escape stack are preserved; no answer/resume/reset/compute action was added.
> 
> Cost/limit: safe operation metadata remains in memory for the window lifetime, intentionally surviving Shell unmounts. This is not request-ID persistence across closing/restarting the app, nor a cross-window lease replacement. The host remains the authority; existing durable checkpoints survive restart independently.

### A39 → R08

**alias/clarification → R08**.

Original wording qualifies retained body; no extra independent decision. Authority `E:/Project/OmicsOps/.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-04-wisp-w04-kernel-inspection/task-2-review.md:23`:

> - **Duplicate terminals across a read-buffer boundary are accepted.** `crates/omicsops-runtime/src/process_backend.rs:510`: `count != available.len()` rejects trailing bytes only in the current `fill_buf()` slice. If a valid inspection JSON frame is padded to put its newline at byte 8,192 (or 16,384), a following duplicate can already be queued in the pipe but outside that slice. The loop breaks, validates the first frame and disarms the health guard. Such padding is explicitly permitted by the existing exact-size frame test. The next execute passes its health check and writes its request before `next_line()` detects the old request ID (`process_backend.rs:349–360`); user code can therefore be dispatched on an already contaminated stream. A later inspect likewise sends before discovering the stale response. Add a boundary regression using the normal 8 KiB BufReader and a valid boundary-sized frame plus duplicate, and make queued extra data prevent successful reuse independently of the current slice. Define the clean-stream/pre-send handling for late frames so detection cannot occur only after another execution was emitted. Any additional execute-path change needs the controller's scope ruling.

> ## Approved legacy compatibility ruling and cost
> 
> Self-review found that re-running `validate_event_chain_v4` on typed events after Store verification breaks legitimately persisted older JSON: deserialization can add default fields (for example `InputRequested.reason`) and change the reserialized hash. `deserialize_event_chain_v4` intentionally validates the ORIGINAL JSON before applying defaults.
> 
> The actual `run_diagnostics_projection_loader_preserves_verified_legacy_event_hash` regression first demonstrated Store success followed by loader failure, then passed after the controller/parent approved this narrow same-two-file adjustment:
> 
> - `project_run_diagnostics` remains strict for arbitrary typed input, including all-chain hash validation and scope/source limits.
> - Private `project_validated_run_diagnostics` is the common projection core. Its trust precondition is a fully validated owned event set. `load_run_diagnostics` calls it immediately after the bounded Store succeeds, without filtering or mutation in between. The private core shares request/scope/schema/count checks and final 8 MiB checking; it preserves the original Store-validated event hashes without typed rehashing. No unchecked public entrypoint was added.
> - Cost: two internal entry routes distinguish typed validation from original persisted JSON validation. Future callers must not call the private projection core with unvalidated events. The typed helper is deliberately more conservative for legacy deserialized events. The loader's original raw byte budget is authoritative; it does not impose a second budget based on expanded current-schema JSON.

### A76 → R47/N01/N02

Original wording qualifies retained body; no extra independent decision. Authority `E:/Project/OmicsOps/.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-04-wisp-w05-context-compaction/task-2-report.md:157`:

> ## Prospective-source ruling, proof, cost and limit
> 
> Controller and parent explicitly approved a private same-byte-shape prospective checkpoint source without expanding the Store API. EventStoreV4 archive_context owns the actual archive UUID and push owns timestamps, so future identity equality cannot be claimed before writing. Those fields, archive checksum and management event bodies are filtered out of model context and replay. The projected reference contains only fixed tool/field names, exact future checkpoint sequence (old head + 2), and a hash-verified 64-hex event hash. Source UUID/timestamp/checksum values influence that hash value but not its serialized width. Native budget closures likewise use the prospective post-management sequence and fixed-width hashes. Frozen system/tool/image/provider fields are retained unchanged.
> 
> The native-growth large-science regression verifies complete serialized request byte equality and ModelUsageRequestMetadata equality (serialized_request_bytes is Some), equality of every non-context field, and context equality after substituting only the future checkpoint hash. Returned reference sequence/hash equal the actual persisted checkpoint; the prospective hash differs and is absent from the actual request context. The actual final strict shaper result equals the final admitted request and passes the unchanged 70,000-byte validator. The fixture uses the ModelPort's complete-request serialization/metadata, not an HTTP provider call. Production byte-budget applicability is a scoped source inference: adapters/src/llm.rs RequestBudget documents and implements compact UTF-8 provider-JSON byte counting, with the same retained image shape/bound; replacing one ASCII hex hash with another equal-width hash does not change that estimator. This is not a claim about arbitrary tokenizers or arbitrary future semantic validators.
> 
> Cost: automatic compaction builds two private management envelopes and clones the captured event list; bounded scientific/code views fit once before writes and again with actual durable references. Full admitted contexts avoid extra validation. Existing lower-bound fallback cost/InvalidRequest ambiguity from fix2 remains. Predictable byte-budget failures write nothing. The existing automatic archive+append path is not atomic: unexpected Store failures/races or provider semantic failures after writing may retain an archive/events. Actual bounded request validation then fails and no inadmissible or prospective request is returned for sending. This does not promise rollback or replace future Task3's manual atomic transaction with preallocated actual references.

> ## Narrow approved image-validation ruling
> 
> The actual image-host regression exposed an existing incompatibility: `DesktopModelPortV4::validate_request` used `prepare_request(..., false)` and emitted empty base64 image content. Existing provider budget parsing rejected that placeholder as `request budget: remote or malformed image URL is not supported`, although real-byte usage metadata succeeded. The controller explicitly approved the same-file fix before editing the method.
> 
> Validation now uses `prepare_request(..., true)` with the existing verified image reader. An early known-image-budget check retains fail-closed unknown-cost rejection before disk access. No image reader or transport changes. Cost: an additional verified local image read in validation; a changed or missing image conservatively refuses admission. Metadata still measures real payload bytes, never a placeholder. This adds no network/provider request or keyring persistence. No filesystem/keyring/SQLite cross-domain atomicity is claimed; normal resume still re-admits after new answers/rules/state changes.

### O-W09T4-CONSUMERS — four report considerations

W09 Task4 added no formal Ruling marker. These are consumer requirements/practical maintenance considerations, not four formal decisions or independently recorded controller costs. Origin: W09 task-4-report.md Implemented behavior / Final modal review corrections, closed source2649097/docsf93878c. Original considerations retained in full:

- **Consumer contract and maintenance consideration — compare saved-selection identity by its fields.** The selection tuple is compared by its four ordered scalar fields, rather than JSON serialization of an object whose property order can vary. This accepts a field-equivalent native response with reordered keys and prevents a valid report/receipt being discarded. Cost: the UI identity comparator must stay aligned with the four-field wire tuple. Source authority: W09 Task 4 report, “Implemented behavior” and “Final modal review corrections”; actual failing regression `task-4-selfreview-red.log` and focused fix evidence.
- **Consumer contract and maintenance consideration — evidence generations are isolated from publication writes.** Project/publication/revision/hash changes, reload, accepting a saved revision, creating a draft, closing and unmount invalidate pending evidence and clear its result/error/cancel state. Late native completions are discarded by generation plus saved tuple. Evidence pending state does not take over the uncertain-write lock or dirty guard; closing a pending export hides its eventual response and makes no claim that the native chooser/write was cancelled. Cost: a late native response can finish without being shown after navigation/close; no cancellation guarantee is invented. Source: `task-4-report.md`, “Implemented behavior”; reviewed against the task brief/context and shared spec.
- **Consumer contract and maintenance consideration — overlay admission and DOM order follow the active Escape layer.** A leave decision blocks starting evidence behind it. The evidence result stays mounted before existing local picker/snapshot/source/leave dialogs in DOM; a later picker at the same backdrop z-index then owns both visual top order and the window Escape top layer. One immediate Escape closes only that picker and leaves the report visible; a higher-z dirty leave decision similarly closes first and preserves report/draft. No report suppression or general overlay framework was added. Cost: result JSX ordering and the local-dialog admission guard jointly preserve the existing overlay stack behavior. Source: final component tests and `task-4-report.md`, “Final modal review corrections”; RED/GREEN records below.
- **Consumer contract and maintenance consideration — do not claim scientific validation from readiness or package hashes.** Report labels retain `NotChecked` / `NotExecuted`, and distinguish stored/reference/file/manifest/ZIP hash scopes. Export rechecks after the save chooser; a prior readiness observation is not the package snapshot. Cost: users may see a new export result differing from an earlier readiness observation; the UI communicates that boundary. This is consumer behavior consistent with the reviewed W09 native contract, not a new native policy.

### POST47-R01 — formal product ruling

Authority .superpowers/sdd/2026-10-04-wisp-w11-migration-preview/progress.md:35. Exact original paragraph with original reason/cost where stated:

> Ruling: W11 ScientificMetadata exposes `MigrationCategorySummaryV1.kind_counts: Vec<MigrationKindCountV1>` with pure deny_unknown_fields `MigrationKindCountV1 { kind: String, record_count: u64 }`. No arbitrary stored kind/metadata labels are emitted. — The binding specification requires per-kind counts, but aggregate record_count and bounded individual examples cannot express those counts; duplicate category entries or overloaded labels would obscure the contract. — Cost: one public pure DTO field/type and exact serde/default/contract tests, followed by stable-digest and TS/UI producer-consumer updates in Tasks2/3; no persistence or new framework.
